**[00:15:00.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000077)  
ang patuloy na matinding pagbaha sa Central, Southern, Northern Luzon,

**[00:15:07.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000078)

**[00:15:14.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000079)

**[00:15:33.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000082)

**[00:15:39.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000083)

**[00:15:44.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000084)

**[00:15:49.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000085)

**[00:15:54.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000086)

**[00:15:58.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000087)

**[00:16:02.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000088)

**[00:16:11.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000089)

**[00:16:18.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000090)

**[00:16:28.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000091)

**[00:16:30.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000092)

**[00:16:35.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000093)

**[00:16:37.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000094)

**[00:16:44.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000095)

**[00:16:47.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000096)

**[00:16:53.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000097)

**[00:16:58.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000099)

**[00:17:01.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000100)

**[00:17:08.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000101)

**[00:24:01.140] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000199)  
Ten-wheeler truck na po nakakapasok.

**[00:24:03.220] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000200)  
Ang savings napakalaki.

**[00:24:05.260] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000201)  
So it is really a big help by this agency

**[00:24:09.480] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000202)  
and it's helping us grow as an agricultural province

**[00:24:14.420] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000203)  
and it is contributing to the GDP growth of the country.

**[00:24:18.140] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000204)  
Kung ma-replicate lang po namin yung ginawa po ng DAR

**[00:24:20.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000205)  
all over the country doon sa Bukidnon,

**[00:24:24.160] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000206)  
talagang walang mahirap na magsasaka.

**[00:24:26.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000207)  
So I salute

**[00:24:40.960] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000208)  
these men and women of here in DAR today

**[00:24:42.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000209)  
para madagdagan po ang budget ng DAR.

**[00:24:46.840] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000210)  
I have nothing else to say but good things.

**[00:24:50.940] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000211)  
At baka sabihin na sobrang sip-sip na ako

**[00:24:52.600] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000212)  
sa kaibigan ko, si Secretary Estrella.

**[00:24:54.980] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000213)  
So I am here for my...

**[00:24:56.700] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000214)  
for my full support of your budget

**[00:24:58.820] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000215)  
and hopefully we can add even more.

**[00:25:00.760] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000216)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:25:01.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000217)  
Thank you, Senator Migs Subiri.

**[00:25:05.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000218)  
Palagay ko hindi lang magsasaka

**[00:25:06.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000219)  
ang gumanda ang buhay,

**[00:25:09.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000220)  
pati yung kabayo.

**[00:25:11.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000221)  
Sa ten-wheeler truck na siya sinasakay.

**[00:25:17.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000222)  
Thank you very much.

**[00:25:19.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000223)  
We now move to our Chairman of the Committee on Finance

**[00:25:21.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000224)  
for his opening statement.

**[00:25:22.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000225)  
Go ahead.

**[00:25:23.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000226)

**[00:25:24.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000227)  
I have no opening statement.

**[00:25:26.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000228)  
Anyway,

**[00:25:26.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000229)  
just here to, of course,

**[00:25:28.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000230)  
to hear the, of course,

**[00:25:32.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000231)

**[00:25:34.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000232)

**[00:25:37.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000233)

**[00:25:40.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000234)  
So we'll do our best,

**[00:25:41.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000235)  
although we know for a fact,

**[00:25:43.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000236)

**[00:25:46.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000237)

**[00:25:49.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000238)  
Pero ganun pa man,

**[00:25:50.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000239)

**[00:25:52.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000240)

**[00:25:54.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000241)  
assess

**[00:25:56.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000255)  
kung ano ba yung mga programs

**[00:25:58.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000256)  
that will really need to be prioritized.

**[00:26:01.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000257)  
Again, nice to have you here,

**[00:26:03.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000258)  
Secretary,

**[00:26:04.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000259)  
and the rest of the DAR family.

**[00:26:07.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000260)  
So I'm here to listen,

**[00:26:09.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000261)  
Mr. Chair.

**[00:26:10.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000262)  
Thank you.

**[00:26:11.680] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000263)  
Okay, comment.

**[00:26:12.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000264)  
Go ahead, Major.

**[00:26:13.240] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000265)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:26:14.300] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000266)  
Alam nyo po, mga kababayan,

**[00:26:15.680] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000267)  
mga kapatid ko dito,

**[00:26:16.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000268)

**[00:26:19.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000269)  
dalawa sila eh.

**[00:26:20.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000270)  
The presiding officer of the impeachment court,

**[00:26:22.700] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000271)  
which is Senator Chiz Escudero,

**[00:26:24.800] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000272)  
and the Chairman of Finance,

**[00:26:26.340] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000273)  
Senator JV.

**[00:26:28.420] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000274)  
Naawa na ako dyan.

**[00:26:29.420] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000275)  
Pati love life niya na,

**[00:26:30.600] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000276)  
na-sacrifice na.

**[00:26:32.020] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000277)  
Kaya naawa ako dyan.

**[00:26:34.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000278)  
Pero I just wanted to comment.

**[00:26:36.420] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000279)  
Naawa ako kay Senator JV.

**[00:26:37.940] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000280)  
Pero we'll do our best to help him

**[00:26:39.280] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000281)  
para matulungan kayo.

**[00:26:40.620] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000282)  
Thank you very much.

**[00:26:41.420] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000283)  
Thank you very much, Chairman.

**[00:26:42.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000284)  
Thank you.

**[00:26:43.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000285)  
And before we proceed,

**[00:26:44.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000286)  
we'd like to also apologize for the delay.

**[00:26:47.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000287)  
We were supposed to meet at 2 o'clock,

**[00:26:49.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000288)  
but patong-patong talaga yung aming trabaho ngayon sa Senate.

**[00:26:55.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000289)  
Napansin nyo siguro,

**[00:26:56.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000290)  
puno rin ang Senate because the committee rooms

**[00:27:00.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000291)  
are also being occupied by parties to the impeachment.

**[00:27:06.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000292)  
The lawyers of the respondent and the lawyers for the prosecution.

**[00:27:12.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000293)  
So medyo mabigat.

**[00:27:15.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000294)  
So we apologize for the delay

**[00:27:17.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000295)  
and we hope to be able to make it up by facilitating the presentation.

**[00:27:26.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000296)  
So Secretary Conrad,

**[00:27:27.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000297)  
you have the floor.

**[00:27:29.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000298)  
Would you like to direct us to whoever is going to make the presentation?

**[00:27:34.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000299)  
Oh, okay.

**[00:27:35.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000300)  
Go ahead, sir.

**[00:27:36.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000301)  
You have the floor.

**[00:27:38.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000302)  
Sa ating kagalang-galang na chairman ng Committee on Agriculture,

**[00:27:46.340] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000303)  
Agrarian Reform and Environment.

**[00:27:50.240] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000304)  
At ang aking schoolmate sa LaSalle Green Hills,

**[00:27:56.100] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000305)  
at mas magandang lalaki po sa akin ng isang paligo lamang.

**[00:28:02.820] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000306)  
Idol ko ho to sa agrikultura.

**[00:28:04.960] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000307)  
Gusto ko ho paglaki ko katulad ko siya.

**[00:28:08.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000308)  
At ganon din po ang ating kagalagalang na majority floor leader

**[00:28:12.720] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000309)  
na matagal ko na hong kilala.

**[00:28:16.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000310)  
In fact, noong NIA administrator pa lang ako,

**[00:28:21.600] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000311)  
ay nag-inaugurate po kami ng electrification project.

**[00:28:26.100] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000312)  
Sa isang liblib na barangay at first term ho na congressman niya,

**[00:28:33.100] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000313)  
eh may nakisakay pong buntis sa sama ho ng daan noon sa bukid noon.

**[00:28:40.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000314)  
Sa haba ng biyahe, nanganak po yung buntis doon sa likod.

**[00:28:45.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000315)  
True story.

**[00:28:46.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000316)  
Oo, true story.

**[00:28:48.720] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000317)  
At noon po kasi iba pa ho yung kanyang love life.

**[00:28:53.320] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000318)  
Ang kanyang girlfriend ho noon ay iba.

**[00:28:56.100] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000319)  
E babae ho yung pinanganak.

**[00:28:58.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000320)  
Kaya ipinangalan po doon sa dati niyang girlfriend, yung naging anak noong buntis.

**[00:29:03.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000321)  
If you, Vina ang ano noon.

**[00:29:09.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000322)  
True story po yun.

**[00:29:11.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000323)  
Sino yung Vina?

**[00:29:19.760] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000324)  
Huwag na ho at baka ho merong hindi na ka-insure, biglang madali.

**[00:29:26.100] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000325)  
Sa ating Senate Majority Floor Leader, Sen. Juan Miguel Migs Zubiri.

**[00:29:33.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000326)  
At ganun din po ang ating magiting na senador,

**[00:29:40.100] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000327)  
ang ating committee at chairman ng committee on finance po.

**[00:29:46.160] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000328)  
At kami po ay nagsusubscribe.

**[00:29:48.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000329)  
Alam niyo po, kami po ay lumuluhod sa inyo, Sen. JV Ejercito.

**[00:29:56.100] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000330)  
Kung naniniwala po kami sa Golden Rule po.

**[00:30:00.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000332)  
that he who has the gold makes the rules.

**[00:30:02.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000333)  
Eh, kayo ang Committee on Finance,

**[00:30:05.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000334)  
kaya po lumulod din ho kami sa inyo.

**[00:30:09.240] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000335)  
At sa lahat-lahat po ng mga kagalang-galang

**[00:30:12.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000336)  
na mga kasama natin sa pagsisilbi sa ating bansa,

**[00:30:17.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000337)  
pipilitin po namin na makapagbigay.

**[00:30:21.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000338)  
Ito po ang mandate natin,

**[00:30:24.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000339)  
taon-taon na nagpe-presenta tayo

**[00:30:27.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000340)  
sa magiging sponsor natin sa plenary

**[00:30:30.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000341)  
ng budget ng iba't-ibang ahensya.

**[00:30:34.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000342)  
At ngayon po, naririto kami.

**[00:30:36.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000343)  
Sana mabigyan namin ng magandang paliwanag

**[00:30:42.720] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000344)  
ang inyo pong Committee, Mr. Chairman,

**[00:30:46.820] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000345)  
tungkol sa budget ng Department of Agrarian Reform

**[00:30:51.600] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000346)  
at pipilitin po namin na makompleto po ito

**[00:30:57.100] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000347)  
kahit na po.

**[00:30:57.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000348)  
Medyo puyat na puyat po kami

**[00:31:00.220] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000349)  
sa kapapanood ng Samoan Someday.

**[00:31:04.300] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000350)  
Malapit na po kasing matapos eh.

**[00:31:06.320] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000351)  
Kaya po, exciting na po.

**[00:31:08.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000352)  
Kaya nanonood kami yan.

**[00:31:09.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000353)  
Marami pong puyat dito sa kakapanood nun eh.

**[00:31:15.160] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000354)  
Kaya po, allow me.

**[00:31:17.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000355)  
Kung pwede, panuorin po din ninyo yung

**[00:31:19.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000356)  
Honored I Matter.

**[00:31:24.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000357)  
Nag-plug na rin eh.

**[00:31:27.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000358)  
Sige nga.

**[00:31:30.920] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000359)  
Manonood din po kami.

**[00:31:32.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000360)  
O bago po nung plenary namin dito,

**[00:31:34.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000361)  
manonood na po kami nun.

**[00:31:38.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000362)  
Well, seriously, it is with utmost respect

**[00:31:41.760] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000363)  
and gratitude that we present the Department

**[00:31:44.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000364)  
of Agrarian Reform's proposed budget

**[00:31:48.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000365)  
for fiscal year 2027.

**[00:31:52.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000366)  
Allow me to begin with a directive

**[00:31:54.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000367)  
from His Excellency,

**[00:31:57.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000368)  
President Ferdinand,

**[00:31:57.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000369)  
R. Marcos, Jr.,

**[00:31:59.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000370)  
during his 2026 State of the Nation Address.

**[00:32:04.160] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000371)  
Ang sabi po niya,

**[00:32:05.600] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000372)  
asahan ninyo,

**[00:32:07.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000373)  
kukompletuhin na nating ngayong taon

**[00:32:09.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000374)  
ang ginagawa nating pagpapawalang-bisa

**[00:32:12.820] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000375)  
sa mga utang ng ating mga beneficiaryo.

**[00:32:20.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000376)  
This directive gives clear direction

**[00:32:23.500] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000377)  
to the Department of Agrarian Reform

**[00:32:26.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000378)  
to fulfill

**[00:32:26.780] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000379)  
the mandate of the law

**[00:32:27.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000381)  
by completing this year

**[00:32:30.960] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000382)  
the debt condonation process

**[00:32:33.240] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000383)  
for our agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[00:32:36.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000384)  
This is anchored on Republic Act 11953

**[00:32:40.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000385)  
or the new Agrarian Emancipation Act

**[00:32:43.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000386)  
which institutionalized the condonation

**[00:32:46.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000387)  
of the unpaid amortizations,

**[00:32:50.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000388)  
interests, penalties,

**[00:32:51.700] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000389)  
and other related obligations

**[00:32:53.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000390)  
of qualified agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[00:32:57.920] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000391)  
The work of agrarian reform

**[00:33:00.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000392)  
has been a continuing commitment

**[00:33:03.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000393)  
of the Philippine government.

**[00:33:05.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000394)  
On September 26, 1972,

**[00:33:07.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000395)  
Presidential Decree No. 2

**[00:33:11.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000396)  
declared the entire Philippines

**[00:33:13.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000397)  
a land reform area.

**[00:33:16.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000398)  
Followed on October 21

**[00:33:17.960] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000399)  
by Presidential Decree No. 27

**[00:33:21.660] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000400)  
or PD-27,

**[00:33:24.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000401)  
the Tenant Emancipation Decree

**[00:33:27.700] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000403)  
It sought to emancipate tenant farmers

**[00:33:29.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000404)  
by transferring ownership

**[00:33:31.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000405)  
of the land they tilled.

**[00:33:34.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000406)  
More than five decades later,

**[00:33:36.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000407)  
on July 7, 2023,

**[00:33:39.220] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000408)  
Republic Act 11953

**[00:33:41.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000409)  
or the new Agrarian Emancipation Act

**[00:33:44.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000410)  
continued this legacy.

**[00:33:46.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000411)  
And today,

**[00:33:48.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000412)  
President Ferdinand R. Marcos Jr.

**[00:33:51.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000413)  
and I,

**[00:33:52.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000414)  
and all of us,

**[00:33:54.660] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000415)  
carry this responsibility forward

**[00:33:56.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000416)  
from land distribution

**[00:33:58.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000434)  
and debt condonation to agrarian justice and the empowerment of our farmers. The years 2022 to 2026

**[00:34:08.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000435)  
may aptly be described

**[00:34:10.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000436)  
as the banner years

**[00:34:11.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000437)  
for the Department of Agrarian Reform.

**[00:34:14.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000438)  
In 2022,

**[00:34:16.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000439)  
at the beginning of this administration,

**[00:34:19.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000440)  
DAR immediately strengthened

**[00:34:20.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000441)  
the implementation

**[00:34:22.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000442)  
of its core mandate.

**[00:34:25.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000443)  
We distributed

**[00:34:26.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000444)  
21,876

**[00:34:27.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000445)  
land titles covering approximately 25,159 hectares

**[00:34:34.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000446)  
and benefiting 24,804 agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[00:34:40.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000447)  
This represented a 124% increase over the baseline period.

**[00:34:49.320] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000448)  
At the same time, DARA reinforced its legal service system

**[00:34:53.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000449)  
and strengthened its rural development campaign.

**[00:34:57.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000450)  
In 2023, a historic milestone was achieved.

**[00:35:02.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000451)  
Republic Act 11953 or the new Agrarian Emancipation Act was signed into law.

**[00:35:08.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000452)  
A landmark measure that was acted upon swiftly by legislators

**[00:35:14.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000453)  
and received broad support to liberate our beloved farmers

**[00:35:18.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000454)  
from the burden of agrarian debt.

**[00:35:21.820] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000455)  
Maraming salamat po sa ating mga senador.

**[00:35:25.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000456)  
Wala hong kumontra dito at ganon din po.

**[00:35:27.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000457)  
Sa House of Representatives.

**[00:35:31.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000458)  
Ang sinasabi po sa akin, kasi ho, kami po ay nagdi-distribute

**[00:35:37.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000459)  
ng mga certificates of condonation na iparating daw ho sa inyo,

**[00:35:42.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000460)  
mga kagalanggalang na mga senador,

**[00:35:46.500] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000461)  
ang kanilang taus-pusong pasasalamat dito sa batas na ito.

**[00:35:50.780] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000462)  
During this year, the Department of Agrarian Reform

**[00:35:54.040] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000463)  
distributed 69,897 land titles.

**[00:35:57.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000464)  
Equivalent to 84,044 hectares.

**[00:36:03.900] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000465)  
Benefiting 73,399 agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[00:36:09.920] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000466)  
Halos dumoble po.

**[00:36:12.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000467)  
This represented a 396% increase over the baseline period.

**[00:36:18.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000468)  
We recognize the invaluable role of our legislators

**[00:36:21.500] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000469)  
in making this landmark legislation possible.

**[00:36:24.900] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000470)  
Maraming salamat po sa ating mga senador.

**[00:36:27.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000471)  
Mga kagalanggalang na mga babatas.

**[00:36:30.100] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000472)  
In 2024, DAR began the nationwide distribution of certificates of condolation

**[00:36:36.360] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000473)  
with release of mortgage or CACROMs.

**[00:36:40.700] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000474)  
This was a historic first for the Department.

**[00:36:45.220] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000475)  
We continued to strengthen our land distribution program.

**[00:36:49.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000476)  
We were able to distribute 102,536 land titles

**[00:36:56.100] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000477)  
covering the land distribution program.

**[00:36:57.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000478)  
120,772 hectares.

**[00:37:02.040] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000479)  
And benefiting 88,310 agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[00:37:09.300] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000480)  
This accomplishment represented a 581% increase

**[00:37:15.040] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000481)  
over the baseline period.

**[00:37:20.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000482)  
Noon pong 2025,

**[00:37:21.700] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000483)  
the Department significantly accelerated both land distribution

**[00:37:27.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000484)  
and debt condolation.

**[00:37:28.920] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000485)  
We distributed 199,838 land titles

**[00:37:34.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000486)  
covering 302,540 hectares

**[00:37:39.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000487)  
and benefiting 176,202 agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[00:37:47.820] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000488)  
This represented 1,133% increase over the baseline period.

**[00:37:54.500] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000489)  
More importantly,

**[00:37:56.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000490)  
we distributed

**[00:37:57.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000491)  
252,953 certificates of condonation

**[00:38:05.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000492)  
and release of mortgage

**[00:38:06.720] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000493)  
with a total amount condoned

**[00:38:09.220] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000494)  
at approximately 35.69 billion pesos.

**[00:38:14.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000495)  
This brings us to the central priority for 2026.

**[00:38:21.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000496)  
Pursuant to Republic 11953,

**[00:38:24.820] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000497)  
DAR was mandated to complete

**[00:38:26.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000498)  
the debt condolation process

**[00:38:28.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000499)  
by June 30, 2026.

**[00:38:31.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000500)  
And we have fully complied

**[00:38:33.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000501)  
with this deadline, ladies and gentlemen.

**[00:38:36.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000502)  
As to the distribution of certificates

**[00:38:38.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000503)  
of condolation and release of mortgage,

**[00:38:42.040] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000504)  
ang natitira na lang po

**[00:38:43.340] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000505)  
yung nasa BARM area.

**[00:38:46.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000506)  
Ngunit,

**[00:38:47.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000507)  
na-process na ho namin yun.

**[00:38:49.700] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000508)  
Na-validate na namin yun.

**[00:38:51.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000509)  
Yun na lang hong distribution sa BARM area.

**[00:38:54.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000510)  
Minabuti namin natapusin ho muna.

**[00:38:56.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000511)  
Yung eleksyon bago ho namin

**[00:38:58.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000512)  
i-distribute.

**[00:39:03.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000513)  
Next, please.

**[00:39:06.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000514)  
This slide

**[00:39:07.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000515)  
presents the Department's

**[00:39:10.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000516)  
General Appropriation Act Budget

**[00:39:12.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000517)  
from 2023 to 2026.

**[00:39:16.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000518)  
Noon pong 2023,

**[00:39:17.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000519)  
we had a budget of

**[00:39:19.240] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000520)  
9.861 billion.

**[00:39:22.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000521)  
This decreased to

**[00:39:24.200] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000522)  
8.081 billion in 2024.

**[00:39:26.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000523)  
Followed by

**[00:39:28.800] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000524)  
10.442 billion

**[00:39:31.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000525)  
in 2025.

**[00:39:33.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000526)  
For 2026,

**[00:39:35.320] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000527)  
DAR received a significantly

**[00:39:36.700] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000528)  
higher appropriation

**[00:39:38.320] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000529)  
of 16.849 billion.

**[00:39:41.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000530)  
The increase

**[00:39:42.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000531)  
in resources reflects

**[00:39:44.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000532)  
the scale and urgency of the

**[00:39:46.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000533)  
programs being implemented

**[00:39:48.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000534)  
by the Department, particularly

**[00:39:50.660] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000535)  
the continuing

**[00:39:52.500] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000536)  
implementation of the new

**[00:39:54.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000537)  
Agrarian Emancipation Act

**[00:39:56.160] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000538)  
and our

**[00:39:56.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000539)  
broader efforts to strengthen

**[00:39:58.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000540)  
land tenure security,

**[00:40:00.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000541)  
agrarian justice delivery,

**[00:40:02.660] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000542)  
and support services.

**[00:40:04.900] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000543)  
The Department's programs

**[00:40:06.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000544)  
continue to be anchored on

**[00:40:08.340] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000545)  
three fundamental pillars.

**[00:40:10.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000546)  
Yan ho, ang land tenure security,

**[00:40:13.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000547)  
ang agrarian

**[00:40:14.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000548)  
justice delivery, at ang

**[00:40:16.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000549)  
support services po.

**[00:40:18.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000550)  
As of August 12,

**[00:40:20.200] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000551)  
2026, under the Marcos administration

**[00:40:22.760] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000552)  
alone,

**[00:40:24.200] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000553)  
from July 2022,

**[00:40:26.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000554)  
to August 12, 2026,

**[00:40:28.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000555)  
DAR has distributed

**[00:40:30.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000556)  
410,179

**[00:40:33.780] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000557)  
titles covering

**[00:40:36.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000558)  
556,088

**[00:40:39.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000559)  
hectares and

**[00:40:40.600] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000560)  
benefiting 376,738

**[00:40:44.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000561)  
agrarian reform

**[00:40:45.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000562)  
beneficiaries.

**[00:40:47.820] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000563)  
The annual figures

**[00:40:49.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000564)  
show a sustained

**[00:40:51.160] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000565)  
upward trajectory.

**[00:40:53.200] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000566)  
Ayan po, nakikita naman po

**[00:40:54.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000567)  
natin sa screen.

**[00:40:56.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000568)  
Eh, pataas po nang pataas

**[00:40:58.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000569)  
ang aming mga na

**[00:41:00.300] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000570)  
na didistribute.

**[00:41:02.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000571)  
Yun ho, for 2026,

**[00:41:04.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000572)  
ang target po namin dyan

**[00:41:06.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000573)  
is a little less than

**[00:41:07.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000574)  
400,000

**[00:41:10.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000575)  
hectares.

**[00:41:13.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000576)  
A little bit less than

**[00:41:14.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000577)  
500,000 hectares.

**[00:41:17.860] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000578)  
Naka-ready na po yan

**[00:41:19.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000579)  
for distribution.

**[00:41:20.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000580)  
Inihintay lang ho namin matapos

**[00:41:22.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000581)  
ang budget hearing.

**[00:41:24.340] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000582)  
Kaya ako'y natutuwa.

**[00:41:26.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000583)  
At very supportive po

**[00:41:28.320] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000584)  
ang ating chairperson

**[00:41:29.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000585)  
nung sinabi niyang

**[00:41:30.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000586)  
wala na kaming tanong.

**[00:41:32.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000587)  
Pero joke lang pala.

**[00:41:36.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000588)  
Pagkatapos na pagkatapos po

**[00:41:38.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000589)  
ng budget hearing,

**[00:41:39.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000590)

**[00:41:42.220] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000591)  
Maghihiwa-hiwalay ho kami

**[00:41:43.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000592)  
mga USEC-ASEC

**[00:41:45.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000593)  
sa iba't ibang probinsya

**[00:41:46.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000594)  
at meron ho kaming nakahanda na

**[00:41:48.760] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000595)

**[00:41:50.240] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000596)  
na mga 300,000

**[00:41:53.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000597)  
hectares

**[00:41:54.320] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000598)  
until November and

**[00:41:56.500] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000599)  
bago matapos ang taon ho,

**[00:41:58.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000600)  
baka matapos namin yung

**[00:41:59.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000601)  
450,000 po.

**[00:42:03.380] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000602)  
Ang matitira na lang

**[00:42:04.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000603)  
kasi 750

**[00:42:05.340] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000604)  
lahat dyan,

**[00:42:08.240] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000605)  
yung matitira, pipilitin ho namin

**[00:42:10.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000606)  
tapusin by next year.

**[00:42:16.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000607)  
This sustained increase demonstrates

**[00:42:18.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000608)  
the department's determination

**[00:42:19.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000609)

**[00:42:22.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000610)  
and provide our beneficiaries

**[00:42:24.340] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000611)  
with stronger tenureship

**[00:42:26.500] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000612)  
and security.

**[00:42:27.960] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000613)  
Yun pong accomplishment naman po

**[00:42:29.760] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000614)  
sa certificates of

**[00:42:31.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000615)  
condonation and release

**[00:44:36.340] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000678)

**[00:44:38.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000679)  
ay we hired

many

**[00:44:42.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000681)

**[00:44:44.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000682)

more lawyers

**[00:44:50.760] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000685)

**[00:44:52.380] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000686)

na hindi naman ino-occupy,

**[00:44:56.360] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000688)  
hiningi, hiniling puna.

**[00:45:00.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000689)  
Kami sa DBM na gawin na lang hong attorney for at pinayaga naman ho ng DBM. So marami ho kaming na-hire na lawyers at we assigned them in the different regions.

**[00:45:16.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000690)  
At tinulad na rin po namin yung style po ng barangay courts to declug the RTCs, the MTCs, the Court of Appeals and Supreme Court.

**[00:45:36.700] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000691)  
Ganon din yung ginawa namin. Meron na rin ho kaming mga barangay courts na pinag-uusap-usap na lang ho.

**[00:45:44.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000692)  
Para hindi ho dumami ang kaso. Meron nga ho kaming Agrarian Reform Justice on Wheels, ARJOW.

**[00:45:59.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000693)  
Similarly, the Department of Agrarian Reform Adjudication Board or DARAB has maintained a strong record in resolving agrarian disputes.

**[00:46:11.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000694)  
Yan din po sa DARAB din po which is the Department of Agrarian Reform Adjudication Board.

**[00:46:14.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000695)  
At tumaas din po ang aming resolution na performance. From January to June 2026, we resolved 7,396 cases out of 9,874 or 74.9%. As of June ho yan.

**[00:46:41.300] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000696)  
These accomplishments demonstrate the Department's continuing commitment

**[00:46:43.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000697)  
to strengthen the legal foundations of agrarian reform

**[00:46:44.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000698)  
and ensure that disputes involving farmers, beneficiaries, tenancy, and leasehold are addressed through appropriate legal mechanisms.

**[00:47:00.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000699)  
The third pillar is agrarian reform beneficiaries development and sustainability. Under this pillar, DAR continues to provide support services that enable our farmers not only to own land, but to make a viable and sustainable livelihood from it.

**[00:47:14.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000700)  
And one important form of support is irrigation. Katatapos lang ho ng NIA kanina dito. I'm sure they also presented that we have in NIA, pondo po namin 'yon, 'yung CARP funds, pero sa kanila po, sila po ang nag-i-implement, and we are monitoring it also. Wala naman hong nakikitang

**[00:47:44.960] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000701)  
Nakikita nga mga Casper the Friendly Ghost po doon sa kono. Wala ho.

**[00:47:53.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000702)  
From July 2022 to July 2026, DAR has facilitated 285 irrigation projects of which 233 have been completed and 52 are ongoing.

**[00:48:07.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000703)  
These projects amount to approximately 5.02 billion covering 34,711.74 hectares and benefiting 40,775 farmer beneficiaries.

**[00:48:25.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000704)  
Importantly, the majority of these irrigation facilities are solar-powered, providing a more sustainable and cost-efficient source of water for agriculture.

**[00:48:37.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000705)  
We have also continued to improve physical connectivity through farm-to-market roads.

**[00:48:46.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000706)  
These roads help address one of the persistent challenges faced by farmers bringing their produce from farm to market.

**[00:48:56.240] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000707)  
Ako po ay naniniwala doon po sa sinabi kanina ng ating gagalang-galang na majority floor leader na lumaki po ang kita.

**[00:49:06.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000708)  
Ang ating mga magsasaka dahil po dyan sa mga daan na yan at pati sa mga tulay na nagawa po, hindi lang po sa bukid nun yan, sa buong Pilipinas po nangyayari yan.

**[00:49:19.960] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000709)  
At salamat din po sa tulong ninyo, pagbigay ninyo ng pondo sa amin.

**[00:49:25.800] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000710)  
There are also 89 ongoing projects covering approximately 108.351 kilometers and benefiting 26,000.

**[00:49:36.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000711)  
8,953 ARBs.

**[00:49:39.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000712)  
Wala ho doon sa farm-to-market roads namin, wala ho farm-to-pocket roads doon.

**[00:49:47.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000713)  
And kompleto ho yun.

**[00:49:49.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000714)  
In fact, kahit sabi namin, huwag na ninyong hahabaan masyado kung hindi kailangan.

**[00:49:56.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000715)  
What is more important? So that the road will last.

**[00:49:59.860] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000716)  
Ako po mismo naguutos sa mga regional directors, sa mga provincial agrarian reform programs,

**[00:50:06.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000717)  
officers, bantayan nga ninyo

**[00:50:08.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000718)  
at siguraduhin lang ninyo

**[00:50:10.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000719)

**[00:50:13.860] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000721)

**[00:50:16.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000722)

**[00:50:18.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000723)

**[00:50:20.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000724)

**[00:50:38.320] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000733)

**[00:50:40.220] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000734)

And because of this

**[00:51:24.800] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000756)

**[00:51:26.900] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000757)

**[00:51:31.040] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000759)  
We all believe

**[01:01:21.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001008)  
It is recognized as one of the most effective implementers of World Bank-funded projects.

**[01:01:27.600] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001009)  
Ito po yung sa split po.

**[01:01:31.800] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001010)  
This reflects the department's capacity to translate international development assistance into timely, effective, and tangible programs that create meaningful benefits for our agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[01:01:48.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001011)  
We now move to our proposed budget for fiscal year.

**[01:01:51.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001012)  
The Department of Agrarian Reform has a total fiscal year 2027 National Expenditure Program of approximately 16.935 billion pesos.

**[01:02:06.800] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001013)  
Hindi po ganun kalaki ho yun.

**[01:02:11.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001014)  
Actually, it's still the same.

**[01:02:13.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001015)  
Lumaki lang ho dahil dun sa mga ODA namin in anticipation for the overseas development assistance.

**[01:02:21.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001016)  
Let us now break down the 16.935 billion fiscal year 2027 NEP according to expense class.

**[01:02:31.200] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001017)  
Approximately 7.066 billion or 41.72% is allocated for personal services.

**[01:02:42.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001018)  
Another 8.621 billion or 50.90% is allocated for maintenance and other operating expenses.

**[01:02:51.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001019)  
While 1.249 billion or 7.37% is allocated for capital outlays.

**[01:03:02.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001020)  
The largest share goes to MOOE which supports the actual implementation and day-to-day delivery of the department's programs and activities.

**[01:03:12.160] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001021)  
Personal services meanwhile provides the human resources necessary to carry out our mandate.

**[01:03:17.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001022)  
From the central office down to our regional provincial office.

**[01:03:21.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001023)  
Capital outlays support necessary investments that strengthen the department's operational capability.

**[01:03:33.780] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001024)  
Ang amin pong budget, kung titignan po natin kung iahambing po natin sa isang negosyo,

**[01:03:45.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001025)  
mas maliit po ang aming puhunan, mas palaki po ang kita.

**[01:03:49.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001026)  
Kaya kung ito po ay isang negosyo,

**[01:03:51.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001027)  
kumikita po.

**[01:03:56.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001028)  
As we look forward, as we look toward fiscal year 2027, our direction remains clear.

**[01:04:05.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001029)  
Sustain the gains we have achieved, complete our remaining debt condonation obligations,

**[01:04:12.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001030)  
continue land distribution and strengthen tenure security, expand access to agrarian justice,

**[01:04:18.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001031)  
and ensure that our beneficiaries,

**[01:04:22.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001032)  
have the means to make their land productive and sustainable.

**[01:04:26.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001033)  
For land tenure security, DAR will sustain the upward trajectory of distribution of split E-titles and remaining land titles,

**[01:04:36.860] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001034)  
including CLOAs, Certificates of Land Ownership Awards,

**[01:04:41.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001035)  
and continue the implementation of Executive Order No. 75 Series of 2019.

**[01:04:49.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001037)  
We will also complete the distribution

**[01:04:51.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001038)  
of remaining certificates of condonation

**[01:04:53.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001039)  
with the release of mortgage

**[01:04:56.100] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001040)  
in the Bangsa Autonomous Region

**[01:04:58.320] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001041)  
in Muslim Mindanao or BARM.

**[01:05:01.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001042)  
At the same time,

**[01:05:02.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001043)  
we will strengthen engagement

**[01:05:03.780] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001044)  
with stakeholders

**[01:05:05.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001045)  
to accelerate the resolution

**[01:05:07.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001046)  
of issues affecting

**[01:05:09.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001047)  
the documentation

**[01:05:10.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001048)  
of remaining land holdings.

**[01:05:13.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001049)  
Finally, the Department

**[01:05:15.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001050)  
will establish a more accurate

**[01:05:16.960] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001051)  
and reliable inventory

**[01:05:19.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001052)  
and data system

**[01:05:20.360] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001053)  
through comprehensive digitalization

**[01:05:22.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001054)  
of land distribution

**[01:05:24.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001055)  
and tenure security records.

**[01:05:27.220] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001056)  
These measures are essential

**[01:05:29.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001057)  
to ensuring that land distribution

**[01:05:31.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001058)  
is not only accelerated

**[01:05:33.600] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001059)  
but properly documented,

**[01:05:35.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001060)  
transparent, and sustainable.

**[01:05:40.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001061)  
DAR will streamline legal processes

**[01:05:43.200] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001062)  
and reduce delays

**[01:05:44.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001063)

**[01:05:46.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001064)

**[01:05:49.200] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001065)  
or a CONA system

**[01:05:51.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001066)  
to strengthen alternative

**[01:05:52.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001067)  
dispute resolution

**[01:05:54.600] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001068)  
and maximize agrarian reform

**[01:05:57.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001069)  
justice on wheels, or ARJOW,

**[01:05:58.760] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001070)  
to bring free and effective

**[01:06:01.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001071)  
legal assistance closer to farmers

**[01:06:03.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001072)  
and other stakeholders.

**[01:06:05.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001073)  
The Department also strengthens

**[01:06:07.300] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001074)  
its legal workforce to fast-track

**[01:06:09.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001075)  
agrarian law implementation cases

**[01:06:11.380] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001076)  
consistent with its

**[01:06:13.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001077)

**[01:06:15.780] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001078)  
Finally, under

**[01:06:17.300] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001079)  
agrarian reform beneficiaries,

**[01:06:19.200] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001080)  
development and sustainability,

**[01:06:21.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001081)  
DAR will transform

**[01:06:22.660] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001082)  
agrarian reform beneficiary organizations

**[01:06:25.300] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001083)

**[01:06:28.160] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001084)

**[01:06:29.660] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001085)

**[01:06:31.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001086)

**[01:06:32.920] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001087)  
through public-private partnerships,

**[01:06:35.760] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001088)

**[01:06:38.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001089)  
and building institutional capacity.

**[01:06:40.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001090)  
The goal is to move

**[01:06:41.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001091)  
our beneficiaries

**[01:06:42.920] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001092)  
from dependence on government assistance

**[01:06:45.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001093)  
toward greater self-reliance,

**[01:06:48.700] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001094)  
productivity, and sustainability.

**[01:06:52.040] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001095)  
Honorable members

**[01:06:53.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001096)

**[01:06:56.380] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-001097)

the fiscal year 2027

**[01:45:34.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002303)

**[01:45:38.120] DAR Undersecretary for Foreign Assisted and Special Projects Jesry T. Palmares** (asr-002304)

**[01:45:43.120] DAR Undersecretary for Foreign Assisted and Special Projects Jesry T. Palmares** (asr-002305)

**[01:45:47.240] DAR Undersecretary for Foreign Assisted and Special Projects Jesry T. Palmares** (asr-002306)

**[01:45:55.500] DAR Undersecretary for Foreign Assisted and Special Projects Jesry T. Palmares** (asr-002307)

**[01:45:59.160] DAR Undersecretary for Foreign Assisted and Special Projects Jesry T. Palmares** (asr-002308)  
Rate of Return, that all bridges should be validated, checked, whether the hectarage of that area will suit the purpose of building something to connect the other side

**[01:46:10.160] DAR Undersecretary for Foreign Assisted and Special Projects Jesry T. Palmares** (asr-002309)

**[01:46:21.380] DAR Undersecretary for Foreign Assisted and Special Projects Jesry T. Palmares** (asr-002311)

**[01:49:36.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002360)

size

because

**[01:52:35.040] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-002404)  
the

tenants or the
